Findings, verified against the code: **H1** = FB-136 (1): class basics enter only through the deck rule (`rpgkit_rules/src/unit.rs:156`; the classic `(_, None)` arm at :1155 adds none), and only `sunk_ward` plays timeline, so in ashlands, tidewrack and iron_reaches `goblin_brawler`, `goblin_berserker` and `ambush_berserker` lose Sweep Kick (the 48 phasefold fights READY did not name). **M1** an older v13 save whose edited deck holds `order.maneuver: ["flank_cut"]` (copied from the founders' auto order, `emberwake_gui/src/party.rs:426`), or a brawler with `sweep_kick` active, now fails `save::decode` -> `UnitState::deck_errors` (`rpgkit_world/src/state.rs:585`, :639); `VERSION` stays 13, no migration (v13 is unreleased). **M2** R2-CLOSE's feel acceptance is empty for the new content: the feel fights and the frozen-fixture goldens (`sim_golden`, `checks_golden`, `timeline_golden`) hold no class basic, RING, `cinder_spark` Field/Assault or `ROW_GAP_CLOSE` user. **L1** `brimstone_draught` reads "for 2 rounds" but its row has `duration: 3` and the battle log says 3 (`rpgkit_battle/src/command.rs:797`). **L2** `founder_kaelen` keeps `brace` in `extra_skills`, so in classic Kaelen is the only warrior with Brace; the writer refuses a class basic in a loadout but allows it in extras. **L3** the "class basic also in the skill path" warning reads base class rows only, not world-overlay `skill_path`s (latent: no overlay sets one). Big picture: timeline-only features (class basics, Field rows, gap-close) thin classic in 3 of 4 worlds; phasefold (classic ashlands) is the only golden on shipped data; save validity depends on data; the ruleset field table is split between `schema.json` (29 keys) and `ruleset.rs` (22 more).
- **Recommendation**: Build holds the `develop -> main` release PR (the full gate is green) until Design rules on: (1) H1 / FB-136 (1): A accept classic losing class basics (HISTORY row; recommended if classic is retiring), or B give classic units their class basics as known skills (re-record phasefold). (2) M2: release R2 now with the coverage gap logged (recommended), or first add the class basics to the frozen `tl_` decks plus one class-unit timeline feel fight, naming `sim_golden` / `timeline_golden`. Follow-up rows (any order, none blocking R2): M1 deck sanitize on load (drop entries the data no longer allows, warn, fixture-save test), size S; L1 one seed step (text "3 rounds", or `duration` 2); L2 drop `brace` from Kaelen's extras or add a check warning; L3 read overlay paths in the warning; Design's call on classic's future and one ruleset field table.

### FB-139: W2-6 built on branches; the mob growth rate has no home, so stage-scaled levels and XP are inert

- **Spec**: `docs/READY.md` row W2-6 (W.2-S2..S3, E1.2-S1, E2.2-S2, T1.2-S2..S3); `docs/sessions/E_ECONOMY.md` line 12 (`base_level + stage * growth_per_stage`); `W_WORLD.md` acceptance 1; `U2_CLASS.md` item 4 (FB-13)
- **Kind**: limit-blocker
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@76b126b2), follow-up `W.2-S2..S3`, `E1.2-S1`, `E2.2-S2`, `T1.2-S2..S3`
- **Finding**: Built, gated and reviewed (no blockers), held off `develop` so the R2 release stays R2-only: **W2-6a** `claude/w26-a` (E2.2-S2: engine `Hooks::manifest_keys` / `manifest_problems`, `store::set_manifest`, `RpgContent` registers `PurseCaps::KEYS`; an unregistered game.json key is reported, null clears it. T1.2-S2/S3: dialogue choice effect `roll` {stat, dc, key, who?} on a GameRolls `dialogue` stream, drawn only at choice time and saved; `best_stat` reads the standing ratings). **W2-6b** `claude/w26-b` (W.2-S2: kit `battle::begin_world(_with)` -> typed `WorldFight`, the GUI keeps art and logging; headless world-fight round-trip test. W.2-S3 stage part: `world_stage` = `campaign.act` else 0 on `WorldRoster.stage`; `foe_level` / `roster_at` / `enemy_levels_at`). **W2-6c** `claude/w26-c` (SERVICES1: `Services` and `ServiceScreen` in `talk.rs`; the Gallery dialogue / shop entries no longer build a `RegionMap`). No golden moved, so the named `journey_golden` / `checks_golden` re-records did not happen. Blocked part: the stage cannot change a level, because the mob growth rate has no data home (no schema field, catalog value or manifest key), so `foe_level` returns the base level and the W_WORLD acceptance "enemy levels scale by stage" is not testable. Also unspecified: which units scale. `Roster::entry` builds enemies, guests and authored party units alike, so `stage` is read only on the XP side for now. Readings to confirm: (1) `dialogue_roll_die` is a world.json field (default 20, 6..100), not a ruleset number; (2) a `who` not in the active party reads 0 and still rolls; (3) the outcome flag `key` is set true or false; `roll` is allowed on choices only; (4) roll stat names are the 6 primary stats plus `initiative`, `move_rate`, `armor` and derived-stat rows; ratings bar and `dc` go up to 999; (5) `Services` lives in `talk.rs` (a `services.rs` split is possible later), and lore pages stay in `RegionMap`.
- **Recommendation**: name the growth rate's home and which units scale. A (recommended): a per-unit `growth_per_stage` int on unit rows (schema + loader, default 0), applied in `Roster::entry` to enemies only (tier not `hero`, no party guests), so XP and battle level read the same `foe_level`. B: one world-manifest `mob_growth_per_stage` for every foe in the world. Build then lands W2-6 after the R2 release, wiring A or B with the `journey_golden` / `checks_golden` re-records if they move.

### FB-141: W2-6 growth_per_stage as built; one reading to confirm (the level cap)

- **Spec**: `docs/READY.md` FB-139 Option A; `docs/sessions/E_ECONOMY.md` line 12; `U2_CLASS.md` item 4 (@87fc8197 on `claude/w26`)
- **Kind**: suggestion
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@0971f4c), follow-up `W.2-S2..S3`, `E1.2-S1`
- **Finding**: Units field `growth_per_stage` (schema int 0..10, default 0, the writer drops 0; `UnitDef`; content check). `foe_level(level, stage, growth)` = `level + stage x growth`, applied in `Roster::entry` and in `roster_at` / `enemy_levels_at` alike, so battle level and XP weight agree; never applied to tier `hero` or to the encounter's `party` / `guests` refs (`WorldRoster.allies`). No shipped unit sets it, so no golden moved. Not in the spec: the builder caps `foe_level` at `MAX_LEVEL` (10, or the base level when higher), the clamp the sim already uses, because levels above 10 are past the XP curve. The 0..10 schema range is also Build's choice.
- **Recommendation**: confirm the cap at `MAX_LEVEL` (recommended: an uncapped level leaves the XP curve and the stat budget tables) and the 0..10 range, or name other bounds.

### FB-143: R3-CLOSE backend audit: no release blocker; five dormant-feature follow-ups and big-picture notes

- **Spec**: `docs/READY.md` row R3-CLOSE (`audit-build` over `origin/main..origin/develop` @e6450e22, code commit W2-6); rows 20, W2-6; FB-139..142
- **Kind**: feasibility
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@3183eed6), follow-up `R4-CLOSE`, `R3-STAGE-SEED`
- **Finding**: Released as #57 (@8d863f58). No golden or fixture changed in the range, layers are clean, and the schema gains only `units.growth_per_stage` and `world.dialogue_roll_die`. Every W2-6 feature is dormant in shipped data, so none of the follow-ups below can reach a player yet.
  - AUDIT-01 (low): rows without `tier` get two defaults: `stage_level` (`rpgkit_world/src/battle.rs:86`) and `UnitDef` read `hero`, while `roster_at`'s XP weight (`battle.rs:215`) and `WorldRoster::tier` (`battle.rs:118`, morale) read `standard`. Fix: one shared default (`hero`, the schema's).
  - AUDIT-02 (medium, balance): 5 shipped enemies have an explicit `spend`. A stage-raised level adds class growth but no spend points (`rpgkit_rules/src/unit.rs:1251` uses the fixed `e.spend`), while XP and purse weight rise in full. Fix: auto-spend the extra pool, or warn when `growth_per_stage > 0` sits on a row with `spend`.
  - AUDIT-03 (low): a `roll` choice on a dialogue loop can be repicked until it succeeds (T_STORY 1.1 anti-save-scum). Fix: extend `tree_warnings` (`check/dialogue.rs:725`) to unguarded `roll` on loops.
  - AUDIT-04 (low): the whole-folder check (`rpgkit_content/src/store.rs:580`) does not report an unregistered game.json key, which `store::set_manifest` does, so a typo passes `checks` and then blocks the next manifest write.
  - AUDIT-05 (low): `stat_kind` (`check/dialogue.rs:108`) checks derived-stat names against the base catalog, while `member_stat` (`dialogue.rs:185`) evaluates with the world's ruleset and silently reads 0 for an unknown name.
  - Unlogged minor rulings for Design to record: `dialogue_roll_die` schema default is `-1` (inherit from the nearest parent world; effective default 20); in `set_manifest`, `null` removes an extra key and an unregistered key in the file blocks every write; plain `Manifest::set` still keeps unregistered keys (tests only); `PurseCaps::problems` now sees the normalized manifest, so a whole float such as `99.0` is accepted.
  - Big picture: (1) R3 features are code-complete but dormant: no golden exercises stage scaling, rolls or purse caps until data sets them; an asset row seeding `growth_per_stage` on a few campaign enemies and one `roll` dialogue, with a journey that crosses an act, would make the release gate meaningful. (2) The sim (`rpgkit_sim/src/world.rs`, `runner.rs:413`) and host (`rpgkit_host/src/lib.rs:231`) always fight at stage 0; a `--stage N` sim option is needed before E/I balance. (3) Stage is the raw `campaign.act`, while checkpoints default `act` to 1 (`checkpoint.rs:47`): `start_act: 1` scales enemies from the first fight; consider `act - start_act` or state the rule. (4) "Enemy" means `tier != hero`, not `side == enemy`, which is fragile for rivals. (5) `ServiceScreen` is reachable only from the UI Gallery. (6) `apply` recomputes the stage at settle time instead of carrying `WorldFight.stage` in the `Tally`.
- **Recommendation**: queue AUDIT-01..05 as one small kit row in a later wave (AUDIT-02 before any data sets `growth_per_stage`); decide (1)-(3) before W/E/T balance; record the unlogged rulings.

### FB-144: R4-1 U2.3-TIERS slice 1 landed inert; slice 2 needs five rulings and the template values

- **Spec**: `docs/READY.md` row R4-1, FB-78; `docs/sessions/U2_CLASS.md` item 2; `I_AI.md` line 20
- **Kind**: limit-blocker
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@3183eed6), follow-up `U2.3-TIERS`
- **Finding**: Slice 1 is on `develop`: a kit content kind `unit_templates` (`data/catalog/unit_templates.json`, one row per `ai_role`: `id`, `name`, `role`, `base_shares`, `growth_shares`, `origin`; checked like class share rows incl. 6..18 at level 1; editable through `aegis content upsert unit_templates`). `rpgkit_rules::unit::template_for` picks the row for a derived, non-party unit whose tier is not hero (role: unit `ai_role`, else class's); the class keeps gear, path, basics and loadout; a templated unit's stats are `roundi(stat * tier_hp)` and HP `roundi(60 * tier_hp) + CONS*8 + STR*2`. No rows ship, so every golden is byte-identical (gate: 2673 tests, 0 warnings; change-reviewer clean). Build's choice, not in the spec: a content kind (asset-lane editable) rather than a crate-embedded table like `tiers.json`; no unit-row template override; the "growth curve" read as the template's `growth_shares` on the shared budget slope (62 + 5 per level). Slice 2 (rows shipped) moves `rules_golden`, `sim_golden`, the timeline and PHASEFOLD goldens and the journey (maybe `checks_golden`), with wide divergence: max HP mostly within +-5% (minions -4..+3, standard -13..+9, elite -22..+21, boss -20..+29) but `calcified_amalgam` +202 HP (276 -> 478), and a level-3 minion's DEX 23 -> 9, STR 21 -> 8 (scaled minion stats 2-9).
- **Recommendation**: rule on each before slice 2:
  1. Template values. Proposed (count-weighted average of today's non-hero derived units' class shares per role, base x62 / growth x5, largest remainder; all pass 6..18): AGGRESSOR base STR 13, DEX 9, INT 7, CONS 15, PERC 9, KNOW 9, growth STR 2, DEX 1, CONS 2 (18 units: warrior 7, brawler 6, tl_drill 4, mage 1); SKIRMISHER = rogue (base 9/16/9/8/12/8, growth STR 1, DEX 2, CONS 1, PERC 1); SNIPER base 7/14/11/9/13/8, growth STR 1, DEX 2, CONS 1, PERC 1 (ranger 3, mage 1); SUPPORT = priest (base 6/8/11/12/10/15, growth INT 1, CONS 1, PERC 1, KNOW 2). Alternative: the dominant class per role (warrior, rogue, ranger, priest).
  2. Double offence scaling: attack power already takes `tier_power` (minion 0.6); scaling STR/DEX by `tier_hp` (0.4) as well cuts minion offence twice. Scale non-HP stats by `tier_power`, by `tier_hp`, or not at all?
  3. Low stats: scaled minion stats fall to 2-4, breaking DEX move rate, KNOW unlocks and gear requirements. A floor (1? 6?), or scale only some stats?
  4. Role vs archetype: an AGGRESSOR mage (`calcified_amalgam`) takes brute stats. Add a unit-row `template` override (default "" = by role), or role+archetype rows?
  5. Gear modifiers are not tier-scaled (only base stats): intended? And should "non-hero" mean the tier or the side (a named enemy on the hero tier keeps class stats)?
  - Slice 2 watch: `rpgkit_sim/src/variants.rs:70`, `rpgkit_world/src/progression.rs:112` and any Forge enemy preview still read class shares.
  - Docs delta: STRUCTURE `rpgkit_data/src/` add `unit_template.rs` (`UnitTemplate`, `UNIT_TEMPLATES_KIND`, `UnitTemplate::over`; `Catalog.unit_templates` keyed by role); `rpgkit_content/src/` add `check/unit_template.rs` and `source.rs` reading `unit_templates.json` (`UNIT_TEMPLATES_KIND`), tests `tests/unit_template_checks.rs`; `rpgkit_rules` tests `tests/unit_templates.rs`; `games/emberwake/data/catalog/` add `unit_templates.json` (kind `unit_templates`, absent until slice 2). GAME_BRIEF unit rules: the templated-unit rule above. PORT_NOTES: `unit_templates` is engine-only (no Godot original). STRUCTURE is stale on `tiers.json`: it is embedded in `rpgkit_rules/src/tiers.json`, not `data/catalog`. EFFICIENCY repeat finding: a new numeric `pub const` in `rpgkit_rules/src` must go in `LOCKED` / `PROMOTED` or stay private (BASERULES1 scan). HISTORY when slice 2 lands: enemy stats delinked from hero classes; goldens re-recorded.

## FB-145 (answered in READY @dc8bf52a, moved 2026-11-09)

### FB-145: R4-1 slice 2 landed; user ruling: one tier_mult replaces tier_hp and tier_power; duel bands out of reach

- **Spec**: `docs/READY.md` row R4-1 slice 2, FB-144; user ruling 2026-11-09 given in the Build Session: "the tier scaling should be a single tier_mult instead of tier_hp or tier_power ... roughly 0.4-0.6, 0.8-1.0, 1.25-1.5, 2.0-3.0 in order", numbers left to Build
- **Kind**: sim
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@dc8bf52a), follow-up `R4-CLOSE`
- **Finding**: Slice 2 is on `develop`. FB-144 is built as ruled: the 4 `unit_templates` rows (seed `r41s2`; shares round back to the integer points at levels 1-10), `template: "class"` on `calcified_amalgam` and `inquisitorial_purifier` (the second caster: a mage SNIPER that would take archer shares), stats floored at 6, gear requirements (`Equipment::meets_requirements` / `wield_penalty`; the spec's `gear::unfit_penalty` does not exist) and unlock KNOW on unscaled stats, no double tier factor in attack power (the weapon part takes it, the scaled stat part does not), sim / progression / Forge read through the template. Then the user's ruling replaced FB-144's `tier_power` scaling: `tiers.json` rows are `{id, mult, skill_cap}`, hero 1.0, **minion 0.4, standard 0.825, elite 1.25, boss 2.0** (before: hp 0.4/0.7/1.25/1.5, power 0.6/0.8/1.0/1.1; the FB-144 text's 0.6/1.0/1.15/1.3 never matched the table). Templated units: stats `max(6, roundi(stat * tier_mult))`, HP `roundi(60 * tier_mult) + CONS*8 + STR*2`; other units: HP `(60 + CONS*8 + STR*2) * tier_mult`, attack power base stage `* tier_mult`. Formula input `tier_mult`; `tier_hp` / `tier_power` kept as deprecated aliases (no shipped row uses them). Re-recorded: `rules_golden`, `timeline_golden`, `sim_golden`, `phasefold_golden`; the frozen `timeline_kit.json` gained the 4 template rows (W2-5 precedent). Not moved: `checks_golden`, journey, `rules_contract_golden`, `rng_hash`, store resave. Heroes, party and fixed-stat units are unchanged in every golden. Samples (L1 unless noted, HP / melee): Footman 147 -> 186 / 31; Enforcer (elite) 263 -> 281 / 52; Captain (boss) 315 -> 452 / 105; goblin brawler (minion L3) 108 -> 104 / 21; warrior class L5 boss 501 -> 636 HP, melee 111. Test-assumption fixes, not engine bugs: `emberwake_content/tests/ruleset_switches.rs` replays `r41s2.json`; four `emberwake_gui` battle tests pick a step whose target still stands or wait for the attacker's slide (FX1 4.2).
  - Tuning (sim feel timeline seeds=40, party 5, campaign 5; candidates min/std/elite/boss -> feel total, party line/elite/boss %, campaign clears arms/gate): midpoints 0.5/0.9/1.4/2.5 -> 31.5, 75/75/25, 0/2, 1/2; 0.5/0.85/1.3/2.0 -> 34.5, 75/100/75; 0.4/0.8/1.25/2.0 -> 60.5, 100/100/75, 2/2, 2/2; 0.5/0.825/1.25/2.0 -> 61, 92/92/83, 1/6, 4/6; **picked 0.4/0.825/1.25/2.0 -> 61, 92/92/92, 3/6, 5/6**. Every tier sits at the low end of its range.
  - Out of reach under the ruled ranges: the R2-CLOSE feel bands (76% overall, duels 62.5-65%). `tl_duel_armour` (L1 hero vs boss) is 0% at any boss value >= 2.0, `tl_duel_windup` (vs elite) peaks at 17.5% at 1.25, so feel tops out near 61%. Party elite and boss win rates sit above their 50-75 / 30-50 bands at every value that keeps the campaign winnable. Standard 0.825 sits just below a cliff (the reach duel is 100% at 0.825, 5% at 0.85).
  - The floor of 6 flattens minions at 0.4: most minion stats hit 6, so low-level minions all start at 84 HP in phasefold.
  - Generic sim class foes (`sime_<class>_<tier>`) and rules-golden class rows now play their role's template (a mage foe takes SNIPER shares), since only shipped units carry `template: "class"`.
  - Open builder readings: weapon threshold tiers (`Equipment::tier_met`) read scaled stats; a `derived_stats` formula using a stat and `tier_mult` would scale a templated unit twice (no shipped row does).
- **Recommendation**: Design records the ruling (decision row, GAME_BRIEF, STRUCTURE / PORT_NOTES `tiers.json` rows `{id, mult, skill_cap}`, HISTORY: "`tier_hp` and `tier_power` merged into one `tier_mult` (user 2026-11-09); slice 2's `tier_power` scaling and FB-78's separate `tier_hp` superseded; re-recorded rules, timeline, sim, phasefold goldens"). For R4-CLOSE's balance pass, decide: (1) re-target the elite/boss duel bands, or treat L1 solo-vs-elite/boss as intended losses; (2) the party elite/boss bands; (3) a lower floor for minions, or minion 0.5 for variety; (4) whether weapon threshold tiers read unscaled stats; (5) `template: "class"` on generic sim caster foes. Kit README already carries the slice-2 paragraph.

AUDIT-04 engine `aegis_content::store::manifest_key_problems(hooks, m)` shared by `set_manifest` and the kit's whole-folder check. AUDIT-05 `stat_kind` accepts a derived stat only if the world's ruleset keeps it (same lookup as `member_stat`). L3 `unit::basics_in_path_warnings` also on world `classes` overlay paths. M1 `sanitize_deck`: `save::decode` drops saved deck entries the data disallows, with a load warning (shape damage still refused; a still-invalid deck resets to the automatic deck).
  - AUDIT-06 (folded in from the R4-CLOSE audit; spec gap): AUDIT-03 as specced accepted an `unless` on the roll's own `key` as a guard, but a roll sets `key` false on a failure and an unset flag reads false, so the choice stays open after a failed roll and can be rerolled until it succeeds. Only an `unless` on a flag that a `set_flag` of the choice or node sets counts now, and not a `set_flag` of the roll's own key (the roll overwrites it); the warning reads "guard it with a set_flag the choice sets and an unless on that flag." Design: record this as the guard rule (or choose a runtime rule: a roll choice hides once picked).
  - M2: the frozen `timeline_kit.json` gains the CLASS-BASICS rows (class `basics` on 7 classes; `brawler.skill_path` and the `brawler` unit loadout drop `sweep_kick`; `sweep_kick` level 2 -> 1; 3 `cyclone_kick` rank rows lose `requires`; timeline ruleset basics drop `flank_cut`; skill rows `brace`, `disengage_step`, `tend_wounds`, `cinder_spark`); `sim_data.json` holds no decks, so it is unchanged. The class-unit fight is the existing `goblin_skirmish`, in `timeline_golden` but **not the default feel set** (user 2026-11-09: feel stays the 5 `tl_` fights; `goblin_skirmish` runs with `enc=`).
  - Verification: `rpgkit_world/tests/save_gates.rs` (autosave slot 0, manual slots 1..5, keyed act-start save: round-trip byte-identical, `pouch_packed` and the `GameRolls` `dialogue` stream continue the same draws); `emberwake_host/tests/rules_cli.rs` (`aegis rules` all rows ok on shipped data; `--contract` matches the contract golden); `emberwake_battle/tests/step_events.rs` (the step stream over `tl_skirmish` and `goblin_skirmish`) and an `emberwake_gui` FX delivery test (`fx::act_kind` now shared with `popped_impact`). No bugs found.
  - Goldens re-recorded (intended, all from M2 because `timeline_kit.json` is the shared catalog): `timeline_golden` (+3 `goblin_skirmish` fights; the 15 `tl_` fights byte-identical); `rules_golden` (brawler rows only: goblin_brawler, goblin_berserker, enemy_brawler, ambush_berserker, brawler, `class:brawler:*`; R4-2's acceptance did not name it); `sim_golden` (every run that fights a brawler moved, so rows move across classes: party line 4.5 -> 5.0 rounds, boss clear 20% -> 10%, role deltas for ranger/priest/rogue/mage, steel-gate campaign 0/1 -> 1/1, one priest KPI row 100 -> 0; a brawler-only variant kit reproduces it exactly). Not moved: `phasefold_golden`, `checks_golden`, journey, `rules_contract_golden`, `rng_hash`, store resave.
  - R4 balance (on `claude/r4-2`): feel 61% (5 `tl_` fights, 40 seeds; windup 17.5%, armour 0% as intended losses; reach 100, flank 100, skirmish 87.5); party (12 seeds) mob 100, line 92, wave 100, elite 92, boss 92 (party HP after the boss 0.31); campaign arms 3/6, gate 5/6 (party HP after the boss 21% / 57%). All inside the FB-145 bands; no regression vs R4-1. `goblin_skirmish` alone: 17.5% won, 20 of 40 capped, 5 fled: a stalemate from goblin kite/flee against the round cap, not a hard fight.
- **Recommendation**: Design applies the docs delta and rules on the follow-ups:
  - Docs delta: engine README `aegis_content::store::manifest_key_problems`; kit README `DEFAULT_TIER` / `unit_tier`, `raise_level`, `sanitize_deck`, the roll-loop guard rule; GAME_BRIEF checks (roll-loop warning and guard, derived stat must be in the world's ruleset, overlay class-basic warning, unregistered game.json key is a problem; saved deck entries dropped on load); STRUCTURE / kit README save gates (C27) and step stream (C28) tests; HISTORY: "R4-2: tierless rows read `hero` everywhere (loot cap and sim were `standard`); saved deck entries the data disallows are dropped with a warning; roll-loop guard = set_flag + unless (AUDIT-06); frozen `timeline_kit.json` gains CLASS-BASICS rows; re-recorded `timeline_golden`, `rules_golden`, `sim_golden` (brawler runs)".
  - Follow-ups from the audit (none hold R4): AUDIT-07 (low) the sim's `elevel=` copy path (`rpgkit_sim/src/runner.rs:233`) skips `raise_level`, so the 5 explicit-`spend` rows leave points unallocated when shifted; bundle with `R3-STAGE-SEED`. AUDIT-08 (low) M1 covers `deck` only: a stale loadout family, `loadout_ranks` entry or `can_wear` failure still refuses the save (`state.rs:764`). AUDIT-09 (low, design call) `goblin_skirmish` fires only Sweep Kick of the class basics and caps at round 6/8; queue a scripted or mage-including class fight if coverage matters, and look at its cap rate. AUDIT-10 (low) the FX test builds `Act` with fixed `magic/on_self/harms` instead of `popped_impact`'s computation; share one constructor.
  - Big picture: the frozen kit is now a hand-curated fork (patched in W2-5, R4-1, R4-2); either name the whole kit-consumer golden set whenever a row edits `timeline_kit.json`, or re-freeze it from shipped data once after R4. Report the two always-lose duels apart from feel overall. The 5 explicit-`spend` rows were authored on class shares and now sit on template bases: re-author or drop the spend. `tier_hp` / `tier_power` aliases and item `buff_stat` (parsed, never read; brimstone says `CONS` while its effect is Empower) are dead weight. A `derived_stats` formula using a stat and `tier_mult` would scale a templated unit twice: add a check before the A/I silos author formulas. Branch pushes get no CI; consider CI on `claude/*` or a `Gate:` trailer rule on golden-moving commits.

## FB-147, FB-148, FB-149, FB-150, FB-151 (answered in READY @5fa8cab0, moved 2026-11-10)

### FB-147: WEBREL1 feasibility check (READY Section 2 row 7)

- **Spec**: `docs/specs/WEBREL1.md` section 7 (READY Section 2 row 7), audited read-only @4093aed3
- **Kind**: feasibility
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@5fa8cab0), follow-up `WEBREL1-E1a`, `WEBREL1-E1b`, `WEBREL1-E2`, `WEBREL1-G1`, `WEBREL1-P1`
- **Finding**:
  1. **Table check**: only 3 compile blockers between `emberwake_gui` and `wasm32-unknown-unknown`; `aegis_core`, `aegis_save`, `aegis_layout`, `aegis_content`, `rpgkit_rules`, `rpgkit_sim`, `emberwake_rules` and `emberwake_world` already `cargo check` clean on wasm32. Blockers: (a) `arboard 3.7.0` (the only crate in the tree that fails: no `platform::Clipboard`); (b) `aegis_ui/src/shell.rs:1456` `Pixels::new` does not exist on wasm; (c) `shell.rs:1489` `ControlFlow::WaitUntil` takes `web_time::Instant` on wasm (**missing from the table**). With those fixed, `emberwake_gui` type-checks with 0 errors. The real cost is runtime: std stubs compile but `Instant::now` / `SystemTime::now` panic and fs / env / TCP return `Err`. Rows: **file reads** live in engine and kit loaders (see 3; `emberwake_gui` runtime hits only `main.rs:85-88`, `gallery.rs:176`); **user files**: `aegis_save/src/lib.rs:29` confirmed, the table misses a second settings writer at `aegis_ui/src/screen.rs:248,285-287` plus `recordings.rs`, `aegis_host/src/playtest.rs`, `frame_tuner.rs:417-448` (dev); `rpgkit_world/src/save.rs:30` (Godot APPDATA path) has no callers; **clock**: 28 runtime sites in 14 files, including the kit (`rpgkit_world/src/new_game.rs:87` `clock_seed`: a New game without a seed would panic), the game (`emberwake_gui/src/battle/{flow.rs:365-449, playback, talks, draw}`, `dojo.rs`, `portraits.rs`) and `aegis_save/src/lib.rs:140`; `web-time 1.1.0` is already in `Cargo.lock` (via winit); **threads/TCP** confirmed (`bridge.rs:20-26`, `shell/headless.rs`, `aegis_cli/src/scenario.rs`, `aegis_core/src/scratch.rs` test helper): they compile and fail at runtime, so they need `cfg`; **process/env**: `emberwake_gui/src/main.rs:76-81` (`current_dir()?` aborts startup on web) and, **missing from the table**, `shell.rs:1380` `process::exit(0)` (Quit); **window**: `windows-sys` already `cfg(windows)` (OK); **renderer**: `Pixels::new_async` (pixels turns on wgpu `webgl` itself); **audio**: compiles, silent without cpal `wasm-bindgen`; **clipboard**: `aegis_ui/src/edit.rs:650-670` confirmed; **bundle**: the table misses `engine/assets` (1.3 MB, mostly tilesets).
  2. **Versions** (pinned): pixels 0.15.0, winit 0.30.13, wgpu 0.19.4, rodio 0.20.1, cpal 0.15.3 all support the web; **no bump needed, so no golden risk**. winit: `EventLoopExtWebSys::spawn_app` instead of `run_app` (`shell.rs:1814`) plus a canvas attribute; pixels: async init cannot run inside `resumed`, so use `wasm_bindgen_futures::spawn_local` and return the surface as a user event; rodio 0.20 does not forward cpal's `wasm-bindgen` feature, so add a wasm-only direct `cpal` dep with it. New deps: `wasm-bindgen` 0.2.129 (already locked), `wasm-bindgen-futures`, `web-sys`.
  3. **Read seam**: about 45 runtime read sites in 13 files: `aegis_core/src/assets.rs` (15; layer resolution and `read_dir`), `aegis_ui/src/theme.rs:823-1024` (6), `art.rs:71,208,430,455`, `audio.rs:117,241`, `tuning.rs:31-36` (hot reload, native only), `aegis_content/src/file.rs:21`, `world.rs:199`, `rpgkit_world/src/data.rs:51,70,80`, `rpgkit_content/src/store.rs:348,517,579`, `source.rs:58`. Many are `is_file` / `is_dir` / `read_dir` probes, so the API needs `read`, `read_to_string`, `exists` / `is_file` / `is_dir` and `list_dir`. One API covers all of them; **rules, sim and battle crates have 0 fs sites**. Kit loader code is touched (`rpgkit_world` data and new_game, `rpgkit_content`), which the spec's E1 layer list (engine only) omits.
  4. **Bundle**: raw data 1.1 MB, game assets 22 MB, kit assets 7.9 MB, engine assets 1.3 MB; gzip -9 of data + game + kit assets is 27.6 MB (data 0.10 MB, game assets 20.5 MB, kit assets 7.0 MB; xz -9 with engine assets 28.1 MB; brotli not installed, expected about the same since the 647 PNGs and 10 OGGs are already compressed). wasm: a 1.6 MB measured release build (0.35 MB gzip) is a lower bound (startup bails at `current_dir()?`, so dead code is dropped); estimate 6-12 MB raw, 2-4 MB gzip. First load about 31 MB: ~25 s at 10 Mbit/s, ~5 s at 50 Mbit/s. Deferrable: audio (kit 5.3 MB + game 4.1 MB, silent until E2 anyway, about -30%) and `backgrounds/story` (VN stages, ~1.1-1.3 MB each; 8.9 MB for all backgrounds) as a second lazy bundle.
  5. **Saves**: `aegis_save::write_envelope` (`lib.rs:165-193`) writes tab-indented sorted JSON; the largest state in the journey golden (64 states, 700 steps) is a 2.9 KB envelope. A long campaign should stay under ~50 KB a slot, so 6 slots + settings + locks < 0.5 MB, far below 5 MB even at 2x for UTF-16; saves are text, so no base64. Risk: itch.io serves HTML5 games from the shared `itch.zone` origin, so the quota may be shared, and playtest recordings with JPG shots could overflow it.
  6. **Cost** (builder runs), all slices can keep **0 golden diffs** (on native `web-time` re-exports std and the seam passes straight to `std::fs`, so `checks_golden` and the store resave keep their bytes): **E1 L** (2-3 runs: arboard gate, clock seam across engine + kit + game, read seam, user store with `aegis_save`'s 14 read / 11 write path sites mapped to keys plus `screen.rs` settings, `cfg` for bridge, headless and exit); **E2 M** (1-2 runs; needs `wasm-bindgen-cli` 0.2.129, not installed); **G1 M** (rework `main.rs:76-130` startup; `emberwake_gui` is bin-only, so add a lib/cdylib start or use the bin; smoke tests need Playwright: the container has Chromium at `/opt/pw-browsers/chromium`); **P1 S-M** (no web job in `.github/workflows` yet; install `wasm-bindgen-cli` and `butler` in CI).
- **Recommendation**: order **E1 -> E2 -> G1 -> P1**, with P1's `tools/fc.sh web` moved into G1 so G1 can be tested. **Option A (recommended)**: split E1 into **E1a "compiles"** (arboard target-gate, `aegis_core::clock` re-exporting `web_time` swapped in at the 28 sites, `shell.rs:1456/1489` behind `cfg`; small, and it makes the spec's E1 compile criterion true) and **E1b "runs"** (read seam, user store, `cfg` for TCP and exit). **Option B**: keep E1 whole. Either way: (1) give E1 a runtime acceptance, since a clean compile proves little when the std stubs compile (a lint: no `std::time` / `std::fs` outside the seams in non-test, non-CLI `src`; or a `wasm-bindgen-test`); (2) let E1 span engine, kit and game for the clock and read sites above; (3) ship audio as a later bundle part; (4) consider IndexedDB, or recordings off on web, given the shared `itch.zone` quota.

### FB-148: PR4-1 part 2 (AUDIT-07, `elevel=` through `raise_level`) parked: it moves `sim_golden`

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR4-1 `R3-STAGE-SIM` clause (2); `FB-143` / `FB-146` AUDIT-07
- **Kind**: limit-blocker
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@5fa8cab0), follow-up `R3-STAGE-SIM`
- **Finding**: routing the sim's `elevel=` raise through `rpgkit_rules::unit::raise_level` changes `sim_golden` run 11 (`party 3 1 comp=warrior,priest enc=world:ash_patrol,world:ash_raiders elevel=+1 pouch=con_heal_potion`), line 10: `world:ash_raiders 100 - 3.0 2.0/2 0.82 1.00` becomes `... 0.81 1.00`. Cause: `ash_raiders` fields `ash_skirmisher`, whose base unit `goblin_skirmisher` has an explicit `spend` (`{DEX: 4, PERC: 2}`), so at +1 level it now gets its extra spend points (the intended AUDIT-07 effect). The row's acceptance says `sim_golden` byte-identical with no `elevel=` change, and names no re-record, so the clause is parked: the plumbing is in (`runner::Def.raise`): a staged copy (`stage=` > 0) already routes its `elevel=` shift through `raise_level`, an unstaged `elevel=` copy does not. The part-2 acceptance test (`elevel=2` on an explicit-`spend` enemy spends the 2 extra levels' points like `raise_level`) was written, confirmed to fail on the parked path, and left out of the landing (review M1: no ignored acceptance test); it returns un-ignored with part 2. Part 1 (`stage=N`) landed with 0 golden diffs.
- **Recommendation**: **Option A (recommended)**: approve re-recording `sim_golden` (run 11 only, one hp ratio `0.82 -> 0.81`) as the intended AUDIT-07 change; Build sets `Def.raise` on `elevel=` copies, un-ignores the test and names the re-record in the commit plus a HISTORY line. **Option B**: keep `elevel=` as a plain level shift (sim-only knob, no spend) and drop AUDIT-07; Build deletes the ignored test. Also confirm one reading (no golden impact): with `stage=` and `elevel=` together, the stage grows the elevel-shifted level and the spend raise runs from the authored level (as built).

### FB-149: PR4-1 `R3-STAGE-SIM` landed (part 1) + `stage=0..3` sweep

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR4-1 (the `PR4-1` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@5fa8cab0), follow-up `R3-STAGE-SIM`, `R3-STAGE-SEED`
- **Finding**: `aegis sim` takes `stage=N` (int `0..`, default `0`; a bad value is refused as "stage must be a number >= 0"). World encounters (party, sweep, campaign: `world::encounter` reads `args.stage`) play at world stage N: an enemy's level follows `rpgkit_world::battle::stage_level` (now `pub`, the campaign's own rule; hero tier, `party` and `guests` never grow; capped at `MAX_LEVEL`), staged enemies are written as copies (`simc_<unit>_s<N>`, `simw_<qid>_s<N>`) built with `raise_level` so an explicit `spend` gets its extra points. `stage=` is listed in `sim help` only (`args::STAGE_USAGE`), since `sim_golden` runs 19-20 print `USAGE`. Tests: 4 new in `rpgkit_sim/tests/stage.rs` (+1 ignored, FB-148). Gate: 2702 passed, 0 failed, 5 ignored, 0 warnings; **0 golden diffs**. **Sweep** (`sim party`, level 5, default comp warrior/ranger/priest/mage, 10 seeds a fight): stages 1-3 are byte-identical to stage 0 because no shipped unit has `growth_per_stage` yet, so the content half needs values before a sweep can show anything:

  | Campaign fight (stages 0-3 identical) | Win % | Avg rounds | Party HP |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | `courtyard_skirmish` | 100 | 1.6 | 0.95 |
  | `pass_ambush` | 100 | 3.0 | 0.94 |
  | `wagon_raid` | 100 | 1.8 | 0.98 |
  | `ashmaw_stand` (arms branch) | 100 | 4.3 | 0.60 |
  | `ashmaw_stand_barred` (gate branch) | 100 | 3.5 | 0.80 |

- **Recommendation**: GAME_BRIEF (sim keys): "`sim stage=N` (0..; default 0) plays world encounters (party, sweep, campaign) at world stage N: enemies grow by `growth_per_stage` through `rpgkit_world::battle::stage_level`, hero tier and guests never grow, and staged copies spend an explicit `spend` via `raise_level`; listed in `sim help` only (`args::STAGE_USAGE`)." HISTORY: "2026-11-10 PR4-1 `R3-STAGE-SIM` part 1 (`stage=N` sim key) landed with 0 golden diffs; part 2 (AUDIT-07 `elevel=` through `raise_level`) parked as FB-148 because it moves `sim_golden` run 11." STRUCTURE: none. Note: `stage=` is one fixed stage for every fight of a run, also in campaign mode, where the real campaign uses stage = act (as the row specifies). For the held content half (`R3-STAGE-SEED`): the 5 fights are all 100% wins at level 5 with this comp, so a `growth_per_stage` pick should be swept at the campaign's actual party levels per act.

### FB-150: PR4-2 AUDIT-08 orphan `loadout_ranks` clause built narrower (literal clause breaks valid saves)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR4-2 item (1) (AUDIT-08: "`loadout_ranks` entries with no matching loadout entry are dropped")
- **Kind**: limit-blocker
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@5fa8cab0), follow-up `POST-R4-CLEANUP`
- **Finding**: the literal clause conflicts with a save invariant. `progression::pin_ranks` (`kits/rpg/crates/rpgkit_world/src/progression.rs:243-253`) pins a rank for every active family of the built unit "so the class default loadout (stored empty) is pinned too", and the party screen's ConfirmSkills with `using_default` (`games/emberwake/crates/emberwake_gui/src/party.rs:1184-1202`) clears `loadout_class` / `loadout_extra` and then calls `pin_ranks`. So a valid, game-written save routinely has an empty loadout with ranks for families in no loadout list; dropping them would change valid saves and break the store byte-for-byte resave. Built instead (in the PR4-2 phase A landing): `UnitState::sanitize_loadout` drops only the rank entries the existing rank rules refuse (`loadout_errors`: basic, unknown or unowned family, a rank the family lacks), with a test that an empty loadout plus pinned ranks loads and re-saves unchanged. `change-reviewer` verified the invariant.
- **Recommendation**: **Option A (recommended, as built)**: keep the narrower rule (drop what `loadout_errors` refuses) and reword the clause in the AUDIT-08 record. **Option B**: drop ranks with no loadout entry only when the loadout is non-empty (an explicit loadout); needs a ruling on whether an explicit loadout may keep ranks of unslotted families for later re-slotting. No golden impact either way.

### FB-151: PR4-2 phase A landed (AUDIT-08, AUDIT-10, tier single-scaling guard)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR4-2 items (1), (2), (3) guard part (the `PR4-2 (phase A)` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@5fa8cab0), follow-up `POST-R4-CLEANUP`
- **Finding**: (1) `rpgkit_world::state::UnitState::sanitize_loadout(d, stash)`, called per member from `save::decode` after `sanitize_deck`: drops a skill-loadout family that is unknown, unowned, listed twice or past the slot caps; drops refused `loadout_ranks` (FB-150); worn gear of an unknown item is dropped, gear in an unknown slot, gear that fails `can_wear`, or an off-hand beside a two-handed main goes to `party.stash`; one warning line per entry; a valid member is untouched. (2) `emberwake_gui::fx::Act::new` and `Act::popped` (used by `battle/playback.rs` `popped_impact` and the fx tests; the step-loop test now passes the weapon's real `magic` flag, an intended tightening). (3) single-scaling guard in `rpgkit_rules/src/derived.rs`: on a templated non-hero (`tier_scaled`), a tier input that shares a `*` / `/` product with a base stat (or a derived row that reads one) reads 1, so `CONS*tier_mult` gives `CONS`, `60*tier_mult + CONS*8` keeps the 60 scaled, `(60+CONS*8)*tier_mult` gives `60+CONS*8`; untemplated units read formulas as written. **Layer deviation (engine)**: the guard needs `aegis_core::formula::Formula::eval_marked(marked, input(name, under))` (game-agnostic; `eval` routes through it with unchanged arithmetic; `change-reviewer` ruled it the right fix, since a static content check cannot express a per-unit guard). No shipped or fixture formula uses a tier input today, so **0 golden diffs**. Tests: `rpgkit_world/tests/loadout_sanitize.rs` (6), `aegis_core/tests/formula_marked.rs` (2), `rpgkit_rules/tests/unit_templates.rs` (+1). Gate: 2711 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings.
- **Recommendation**: engine/README.md `aegis_core` row (:138): add "`Formula::eval_marked`: eval that tells the input whether a name shares a product with a marked name (PR4-2 tier guard)". STRUCTURE tests: `rpgkit_world/tests/loadout_sanitize.rs` (PR4-2 AUDIT-08), `aegis_core/tests/formula_marked.rs`. GAME_BRIEF save: "A saved loadout family, rank or worn item the data no longer allows is dropped on load with a warning (worn gear to the stash); data drift never refuses a save." GAME_BRIEF stats: "On a templated non-hero, a derived formula never applies `tier_mult` to a stat again." HISTORY: none (0 golden diffs). EFFICIENCY (builder tip): "dry-run a kit re-freeze in a scratch worktree (`RECORD=1` on `timeline_golden`) before re-recording".

## FB-152, FB-153, FB-154, FB-155 (answered in READY @738a366b, moved 2026-11-10)

### FB-153: skill content check silently skips template validation when a family head fails to fold

- **Spec**: found in the PR4-2 phase B review (`kits/rpg/crates/rpgkit_content/src/check/skill.rs:665`)
- **Kind**: suggestion
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@738a366b), follow-up `SKILLCHECK-FOLD`
- **Finding**: `entry_errors` runs `validate_template` only `if let Ok(folded) = fold(...)`; a fold error is dropped, so a skill whose rank rows cannot fold gets no template errors and no fold error either. The PR4-2 re-freeze exposed it: the slim `checks_golden` fixture bases lacked the loader-computed `spell_source`, every family head failed to fold, and the golden silently lost its `{jump}` token errors; on develop `shield_wall` and `mend` were already skipped (`guard_rounds` / `position` fold mismatches). The harness was aligned in PR4-2 and all 16 templated skill bases fold again. Shipped data is unaffected (the loader marks every row), but a hand-authored or seed row with a stray rank key would pass checks it should fail.
- **Recommendation**: **Option A (recommended)**: report the fold error as a content error on the entry (one line), so a row that cannot fold is never silently unchecked; moves `checks_golden` only if a fixture case hits it (name it). **Option B**: leave as is (shipped data always folds). Small kit task either way; not queued by Build.

- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@738a366b), follow-up `R3-STAGE-SIM`
- **Finding**: `elevel=` copies now set `runner::Def.raise` (`scenarios::catalog_ids` for catalog and scenario rosters, `world::map_ref` for story characters with extras), so an explicit-`spend` enemy raised by `elevel=` gets its extra points through `rpgkit_rules::unit::raise_level` as in the world; the acceptance test `an_elevel_raise_spends_an_explicit_spend_enemys_extra_points_like_raise_level` is back (not ignored; `rpgkit_sim/tests/stage.rs` 7 tests). **`sim_golden` re-recorded, run 11 only** (`party 3 1 comp=warrior,priest enc=world:ash_patrol,world:ash_raiders elevel=+1 pouch=con_heal_potion`, 6 lines): `world:ash_raiders` rounds `3.0 -> 4.0`, party hp ratio `0.81 -> 0.98`, plus that fight's per-unit lines (`goblin_skirmisher` and the shaman) and its metric count (`68 -> 71`, 0 METRIC FAIL). **Note**: the FB-148 approval quoted `0.82 -> 0.81`, measured before the PR4-2 phase B kit re-freeze (`c49194b2`, which re-recorded `sim_golden` and already set this line to `0.81`); the same change on the re-frozen baseline gives the numbers above. Cause (verified by `change-reviewer`): the only explicit-`spend` unit in `ash_raiders`, `goblin_skirmisher` (L3, `{DEX: 4, PERC: 2}`), fought at L4 one point short of its pool; it now gets `spend_pool(4) - spend_pool(3)` = 1 point by rogue growth shares, and with one seed that point turns the fight (4 rounds, the priest heals 161 instead of 80); `raise_level` differs from the plain level write only in that spend, and a negative `elevel=` behaves as before. No other golden moves. Not covered by a golden but changed the same way: a positive `elevel=` on the other explicit-`spend` units (`enemy_brawler`, `tl_footman`, `tl_footman_b`, `tl_crossbowman`) in catalog encounters and their arrival waves, and `elevel=`-shifted guests (no shipped guest has an explicit `spend`). GAME_BRIEF (sim keys): "`elevel=` copies spend an explicit `spend`'s extra points via `raise_level` (PR4-1 part 2)". ### FB-155: PR4-3 `WEBREL1-E1a` landed (the game compiles for wasm32; one clock seam)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR4-3; `docs/specs/WEBREL1.md` 2.3, 6.1 (the `PR4-3` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@738a366b), follow-up `WEBREL1-E2`
- **Finding**: `cargo check --target wasm32-unknown-unknown -p emberwake_gui` is clean with 0 warnings (a separate `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`, e.g. `target-wasm`, keeps the native build warm). (1) `aegis_core::clock` (`Instant`, `SystemTime`, `UNIX_EPOCH`, `Duration`): `std::time` on native, `web-time 1.1.0` on wasm32 (wasm-only dependency, already locked through winit); swapped in at 34 lines in 25 files (`aegis_ui` 13 in 8, `aegis_host` 1, `aegis_save` 1, `rpgkit_world/src/new_game.rs` `clock_seed` 1, `emberwake_gui` 17 in 13, `emberwake_host` 1; `aegis_host` and `emberwake_host` now depend on `aegis_core`). (2) `arboard` is native-only (target table); on wasm the editor clipboard is in-app only (`aegis_ui/src/edit.rs`). (3) `aegis_ui::shell::open_pixels`: native is the unchanged `Pixels::new`; wasm returns `Err` until PR4-5 `WEBREL1-E2` (the caller's `.expect("pixels")` would panic there, so E2 must replace it). `ControlFlow::WaitUntil` needed no `cfg` (the clock's `Instant` is winit's `web_time` type on wasm). (4) The runtime lint is a Rust test, `engine/crates/aegis_core/tests/clock_lint.rs` (so both gates run it): it walks every engine, kit and game crate's `src/` (skipping `#[cfg(test)]` items and out-of-line test modules; `tests/`, `benches/`, `examples/`, `src/bin/` and crate `aegis_cli` excluded) and refuses `std::time::{Instant, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH}` in any spelling and `web_time` outside the seam; allowed: `aegis_core/src/clock.rs` and `aegis_ui/src/tuning.rs` (it only holds the `SystemTime` that `fs::metadata().modified()` returns for hot reload; no `now()`). **0 golden diffs**; gate 2722 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings.
- **Recommendation**: engine/README.md `aegis_core` row: "`clock`: the one wall clock (`Instant`, `SystemTime`, `UNIX_EPOCH`, `Duration`): std on native, `web-time` on wasm (WEBREL1-E1a); `tests/clock_lint.rs` refuses `std::time::{Instant, SystemTime}` elsewhere in non-test, non-CLI `src`." `aegis_ui` row: "`arboard` is native-only (in-app clipboard on wasm); `shell::open_pixels` (web renderer: WEBREL1-E2)." STRUCTURE: `aegis_core/src/clock.rs` (WEBREL1-E1a clock seam); engine tests `aegis_core/tests/clock_lint.rs`. known-issues row: "wasm: std `Instant::now` / `SystemTime::now` compile but panic on wasm32; read the clock through `aegis_core::clock` (clock_lint fails otherwise)." HISTORY: none. Possible CLEANUP row (reviewer): the lint walks kit and game sources from an engine crate's tests (precedent `aegis_core/tests/clip_windup.rs`); a workspace check beside `tools/fc.sh layers` would be cleaner. Note for PR4-5: `aegis_ui/src/shell/headless.rs` calls `std::thread::sleep` (panics on wasm; fine while headless stays native-only, as PR4-4 `cfg`s it out).

- **Finding**: (1) `aegis_core::vfs`: `read`, `read_to_string`, `exists`, `is_file`, `is_dir`, `list_dir` (`std::fs` on native, `read_dir` order kept, sorting callers still sort; on wasm a mounted `Table` of path -> bytes with derived folders and sorted listings, `vfs::key` normalisation, `mount` additive so a later bundle part such as audio can extend it; PR4-6 fills it). Routed sites, probes included: `aegis_core` 15, `aegis_content` 17, `aegis_ui` 19, `rpgkit_content` 14, `rpgkit_world` 3, `emberwake_gui` 3 (`main.rs:85-88`, `gallery.rs`). Left on `std::fs`: the content writer's writes (`aegis_content` `store.rs`, `manifest.rs`: tooling; their reads use the seam), the tuning hot-reload stamp (native-only), `aegis_core/src/scratch.rs` (test helper), `aegis_cli`, `src/bin`. PNG reads decode from memory and PNG writes encode to memory first (same bytes; a failed encode leaves no partial file). (2) `aegis_save::store`: a `Store` trait (native `Files`, unchanged; web `Keys<LocalStorage>`, keys `aegis/<game id>/<relative path>`, a refused write becomes `io::ErrorKind::StorageFull` and the usual "Can't write" save problem, the old save kept); all 26 `aegis_save` sites and the `aegis_ui/src/screen.rs` settings writer use it (`aegis_ui` now depends on `aegis_save`; `aegis_save` gains a wasm-only `web-sys` with `Window`, `Storage`); `with_store` swaps the store per thread for tests, `MemKeys` is an in-memory backend with a byte quota. Web saves are the same bytes. (3) wasm `cfg`: `bridge::start` returns None; the headless run loop is native-only; recordings are forced off on the web; Quit is a no-op on wasm (a page cannot close its own tab; FB-157). (4) Lints: the PR4-3 scanner moved to `aegis_core/tests/lint/mod.rs`; `fs_lint.rs` refuses every `std::fs` path and no-argument path probes outside the seams (allowed: `vfs.rs`, `aegis_save/src/store.rs`, `scratch.rs`; content-writer files may keep write calls only; crate `aegis_cli` excluded); both lints now skip exactly `#[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]` items and modules (so `tuning.rs` left the clock allow-list). Tests: vfs 4, web_store 5, settings_store 2, fs_lint 5. wasm32 check of `emberwake_gui` clean. **0 golden diffs** (`checks_golden` and the store byte-for-byte resave unchanged); gate 2738 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings.
- **Recommendation**: engine/README.md `aegis_core` row: "`vfs`: the one read seam (`read`, `read_to_string`, `exists`, `is_file`, `is_dir`, `list_dir`; `std::fs` on native, a mounted `Table` on wasm, `mount`); `vfs::dev`: dev files (recordings, shots, dev-tool writes), off on the web; `tests/fs_lint.rs` refuses `std::fs` and path probes outside the seams (scanner shared with `clock_lint` in `tests/lint/`)." `aegis_save` row: "`store`: the user store trait (`Files` native, `Keys<LocalStorage>` web, keys `aegis/<game id>/<path>`; a quota refusal is a save problem), `with_store`, `MemKeys`." `aegis_ui` row: "settings go through `aegis_save::store` (depends on `aegis_save`); agent link, headless runs and recordings are native-only; Quit is a no-op on wasm." STRUCTURE: `aegis_core/src/vfs.rs`, `aegis_save/src/store.rs`; tests `aegis_core/tests/{fs_lint.rs, vfs.rs, lint/mod.rs}`, `aegis_save/tests/web_store.rs`, `aegis_ui/tests/settings_store.rs`. known-issues: "wasm: `std::fs` compiles but returns Err; read through `aegis_core::vfs` and user files through `aegis_save::store` (`fs_lint` fails otherwise)." HISTORY: none. EFFICIENCY tip (builder): total a long test log with `grep '^test result' | awk` instead of reading it.

### FB-157: PR4-4 additions beyond the WEBREL1 text (`vfs::dev`, lint pass-lists) and Quit on the web

- **Spec**: `docs/specs/WEBREL1.md` 2.1, 6.1 (PR4-4); for G1 (`PR4-6`)
- **Kind**: suggestion
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@07f1df35), follow-up `WEBREL1-G1`
- **Finding**: (1) **`aegis_core::vfs::dev`** is a new public engine API beyond 2.1's list: F12 shots, note JPGs, recordings, frame-tuner writes and `ground::compose_file` go through it (`std::fs` on native; every call fails with `Unsupported` on the web, so a dev feature reports instead of panicking). It implements 6.1's "recordings and F12 screenshots off on web" in one named seam the lint sees, instead of `cfg` on about 10 files; `change-reviewer` recommends keeping it. (2) The fs lint passes the content writer's write calls (`WRITERS`; its reads and probes are still linted) and allows `aegis_core/src/scratch.rs` (a test helper). (3) **Quit on the web is a no-op** (the quit flag is cleared and the screen stays; the user closes the tab), which cannot trap the user; a page cannot close its own tab. (4) A web save writes a temp key then moves it over the save, so it briefly needs about twice the save's size in quota (fine under the 0.5 MB estimate; `setItem` is already atomic, so G1 could skip the temp key).
- **Recommendation**: (1)-(2) record `vfs::dev` and the two pass-lists in WEBREL1 2.1 / 6.1 (as built). (3) **Option A (recommended)**: on the web, Quit returns to the main menu (or the menu hides Quit), decided in G1; **Option B**: keep the no-op. (4) Optional G1 tidy: write web saves directly (no temp key).

### FB-158: PR4-8 `SKILLCHECK-FOLD` landed (fold error when nothing else is reported; ranks normalised before the fold)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR4-8 (`FB-153` Option A) (the `PR4-8` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: suggestion
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@07f1df35), follow-up `SKILLCHECK-FOLD`
- **Finding**: `rpgkit_content::check::skill::entry_errors` now reports a family head whose ranks do not fold as one content error, `Family '<id>': the description template is not checked because the ranks do not fold (<first fold problem>)` (test `rpgkit_content/tests/skill_fold_error.rs`, 3). Two choices beyond the row's text: (1) **the error is emitted only when the entry reports no other error**: emitting it always added a restated error to 315 of 409 `checks_golden` skill cases (for example "Heal base belongs to SUPPORT skills, not ATTACK." plus a fold error naming `heal_base`) and caught nothing new; with the rule, an entry that already fails `content check` shows its fold problem on the next check after that error is fixed, so no data set reaches `data ok` with an unchecked template (fold problems reported nowhere else: a rank-2+ unknown key, a rank name that is not the derived name, a template that differs between ranks). `change-reviewer` ruled it within the spec's intent. (2) **Ranks are filled with schema defaults before the fold** (`content("skills", o)`, as `collapse` does): `validate_entry` passes a defaulted head while sibling rows came raw, so a rank missing a schema-default key (a row saved before a field existed, the AUDIT-10 case) failed the fold with "'system' must match across ranks" — silent before, a false error once fold errors surface. **0 golden diffs** (`checks_golden` unchanged), shipped data `data ok` (0 new errors); gate 2741 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings.
- **Recommendation**: **Option A (recommended, as built)**: keep both. **Option B**: emit the fold error always (re-record `checks_golden`: 315 cases gain a restated error). HISTORY: none (0 golden diffs). STRUCTURE tests: `rpgkit_content/tests/skill_fold_error.rs`.

### FB-159: PR4-5 `WEBREL1-E2` landed (engine web start path; minimal example passes in headless Chromium)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR4-5; `docs/specs/WEBREL1.md` 2.3 (the `PR4-5` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@07f1df35), follow-up `WEBREL1-E2`
- **Finding**: `aegis_ui::shell::start_web(cx, stack, options, canvas_id) -> anyhow::Result<()>` (wasm only): winit `spawn_app` on the page canvas with that id (an error if it is missing, not a canvas, or `options.headless`); the game mounts its files with `aegis_core::vfs::mount` first (G1). The renderer starts asynchronously (`PixelsBuilder::build_async` in `spawn_local` from `resumed`, returned as the user event `Wake::Surface`; frames are drawn but not shown until it arrives) on **WebGL2** (FB-160); the canvas is 1280 x 720 CSS px at the device pixel ratio (2560 x 1440 at 2x), gets keyboard focus, and its default actions are prevented (a right click goes back, no context menu); window show/size/placement are skipped on wasm. `Audio::unlock` opens the device on the first key, button or touch on the web (a no-op on desktop and offline), one-shot sounds before it are dropped, the screen's wanted music fades in at that gesture (FB-160); `Audio::live`. **Native change**: a window or renderer start failure now ends the loop and `run` returns `Err` (the only caller, `emberwake_gui` `main.rs`, returns it from `main`: the error is printed and the exit code is 1) instead of a panic. Wasm-only deps already in `Cargo.lock` (4 dependency-list lines, no new packages, no bumps): `cpal 0.15.3` with `wasm-bindgen`, `wasm-bindgen =0.2.129`, `wasm-bindgen-futures 0.4.79`, `web-sys 0.3.106`. Example `engine/crates/aegis_ui/examples/web_min.rs` (no game) and `tools/web/web_min_check.sh` + `.js` (build for wasm32, bind with `wasm-bindgen-cli 0.2.129`, installed by `cargo install wasm-bindgen-cli --version 0.2.129 --locked`, headless Chromium): 26 checks pass at DPR 1 and 2 (frames, canvas sizes, a non-blank screenshot, audio off before and on after the first gesture, key and click reach the screen with the CSS-to-canvas mapping, right click goes back, no console errors); not in the gate (needs the wasm target, wasm-bindgen-cli, Node with Playwright and Chromium, about a minute). **0 golden diffs**; gate 2743 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings.
- **Recommendation**: engine/README.md `aegis_ui` row: "`shell::start_web(cx, stack, options, canvas_id)` (wasm, WEBREL1-E2): the shell on a page canvas by id, 1280 x 720 CSS px at the device pixel ratio, renderer started asynchronously on WebGL2 (`Wake::Surface`), context menu prevented, no agent link; `Audio::unlock` / `Audio::live`: on the web the device opens at the first key or click, earlier one-shot sounds dropped, the wanted music starts then; a native start failure is `run`'s `Err` (no panic)." STRUCTURE: `engine/crates/aegis_ui/examples/web_min.rs` (WEBREL1-E2 minimal web example), `engine/crates/aegis_ui/tests/audio_gesture.rs`, new row `tools/web/` (`web_min_check.sh`, `web_min_check.js`: build, bind with wasm-bindgen-cli 0.2.129, headless Chromium check; not in the gate). known-issues: "wasm: wgpu 0.19 picks WebGPU whenever `navigator.gpu` exists, even with no adapter ('No suitable adapter' in headless Chromium): force `Backends::GL`"; "Playwright `deviceScaleFactor` alone reports `devicePixelContentBoxSize` in CSS px: add the launch flag `--force-device-scale-factor`". HISTORY: none.
