## FB-190, FB-191, FB-192, FB-193, FB-194, FB-195 (answered in READY @ea3bf66f, moved 2026-11-10)

### FB-190: PR5-7 `PK1-S3` data landed (Strain, the crisis, Feed and Vent, test units, the Channel duel preset, `channel` in the timeline row)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR5-7; `docs/sessions/PK1_CORRUPTION.md` sections 2, 2.1, 4.3, 5 (the `PR5-7` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@ea3bf66f), follow-up `PK1-S3`, `PK1-S3-FIX`
- **Finding**: Seed `games/emberwake/data/seeds/pk1s3.json` (15 steps, through `aegis content`): `unit_meters/strain` (0-100, start 0, bar, `delta_max` 50; decay -10 `unless_gained` and -10 `if_guarded`; `skills` feed, vent; bands Dormant 0, Acute Resonance 25: crit +15, `heal_taken` -25, `haste`; Flesh-Tension 50: `stagger_taken` x2, `empower` 25, `fortify` 4; Aberrant Edge 75: `empower` 50; Rupture 100); its `triggers`: the crisis (`round_end`, Strain at least 100: `roll_table` of three weight-1 rows, then `meter_set strain 50`): Unchecked Spasm (`strike_nearest` power 1.0, then `stun` 1 round), Biological Collapse (`ap set 0` 1 round, `bleed` 3 rounds, `rating_set armor 0` for the battle), Flesh Breach (`mark calcify 1`; the stage passive and Stage V turn come with the PK2 track row, PR5-19); and the Aberrant Edge recoil (`hit_dealt` at 75+, `damage pct_of_hit 10, to: self`, FB-174). Skills: Feed (SUPPORT, self, 0 AP, `heal_pct` 4, +5 Strain, `channel.only`), Vent (FOCUS with `round_intent: support`, self, -20 Strain, `hp_cost_pct` 15, `requires_meter {strain, 50}`, `channel.only`; a SUPPORT skill must heal, cleanse or grant), test skills `marrow_lash`, `marrow_bolt` (`channel.only`), `strain_bite` (a Strain rider). Passives `inoculated_test` (the meter, floor 0, the two marrow test skills), `aberrant_strain`. Item `con_quenched_salt` (RARE, `unmark calcify`; no drop or price rows yet). Units `inoculated_hero` (level 2), `aberrant_test` (elite, level 2); encounter `pk1_channel_duel`; Dojo preset `channel_duel` (`wpn_broadsword`, `arm_leather`, the Salt, `inoculated_test`); the shipped `timeline` row gains rule `channel`, `channel_meter: strain`, `channel_rest: feed` (power, cost, `marrow_actives`, `channel_ai_edge` at kit defaults). Four data-reading tests take their expectations from the seed: `ruleset_switches.rs` (replays `pk1s3.json`'s ruleset step last), `channel_tests.rs` (the no-rule case passes `-channel`), `ir21_intent.rs` (138 skills: Feed and Vent derive Stand, the three test attacks Assault; 28 dual unchanged; primary [62, 24, 19, 13, 15, 5], listing [81, 24, 28, 13, 15, 5]), the Dojo quick-setup count (baseline + 1). Content check clean; gate 2903 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings; **0 golden diffs** (`phasefold_golden`, the canary, unchanged). Smoke `sim feel 2 seeds=10 enc=pk1_channel_duel`: 0 of 10 won, median 3 rounds; the auto-drafted hero never Channelled, so Feed, Vent and the crisis did not show; fights end in 2-4 rounds at every enemy tier and level tried (HP decides). The balance run (PK1 6.1 watch list) follows as its own item.
- **Recommendation**: GAME_BRIEF (meters / Channel): "Strain (PK1-S3, data): bands at 25 / 50 / 75 / 100, decay 10 a round (and 10 guarded), the crisis at 100 (one of three, then Strain 50), Feed (+5, 4% heal) and Vent (-20, 15% HP, 50+) as the Channel basics; test hero `inoculated_hero`, test enemy `aberrant_test`, encounter `pk1_channel_duel`, Dojo preset Channel duel." STRUCTURE: `games/emberwake/data/catalog/dojo_presets.json` now ships. HISTORY: none (no golden moved).

### FB-191: PR5-7 PK1 balance run: the test hero cannot Channel, the crisis never fires, Channel dominates elites

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR5-7 (balance run on PK1 6.1's watch list); `docs/sessions/PK1_CORRUPTION.md` 6.1; `PR5-22` (longer fights)
- **Kind**: sim
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@ea3bf66f), follow-up `AUDIT-PR5-BUGS`, `PK1-S3-FIX`
- **Finding**: Build `develop` @c1ca8cc6; probes on scratch copies of the data (`data=<dir>`), 40 seeds, `sim feel 2 seeds=40 enc=pk1_channel_duel ruleset=timeline`, control `rules=-channel`; details in `BALANCE_REPORT.md` (2026-11-10 entry). (1) **The test hero never Channels**: `inoculated_hero` has `deck.active: []` and no `deck.marrow`; Strike is a ruleset basic (no marrow version), the warrior's class basic `brace` is a guard (none), and `marrow_lash` / `marrow_bolt` are `channel.only`, so `ai::channel_row` never sees a marrow version. With `tl_hook_thrust` in its `extra_skills`, `deck.active` and `deck.marrow` the AI Channels: 85% won (median 2 rounds) vs 2.5% for Cold Steel. (2) **The AI's Channel choice is a cliff**: with that skill, `channel_ai_edge` 1.0 / 1.2 give 85% and 1.5 / 2.0 give 2.5% (never Channels); `channel_power` 1.0 gives 2.5%, 1.25 gives 85%, 1.5 gives 92.5% (1.25 clears the 1.2 edge only through armour piercing). (3) **The crisis never fires** (0 in 120 fights, although Strain reaches 100 about 1.0 / 1.6 / 3.2 times a battle): `meter::round_end` runs decay before the `round_end` triggers, so a Cold Steel round takes 100 to 90 before the `at_least 100` check; only a Channel round (no decay) ending at 100+ could fire it, and `channel_row` refuses a round whose Strain would reach the max. The crisis table is unexercised. (4) **Vent**: the AI never plans it (0 uses), so "Vent worth it" and "Vent too easy" cannot be measured by the sim; the hero sits in Aberrant Edge (empower +50%) for most of the fight. (5) **Strain's downside is weak** against this enemy: Strain Bite +15 a hit vs decay 10; the only cost is the Edge recoil, 2.6-3.8 HP a fight. (6) **Inoculated dominance**: in short elite fights Channel wins 85% vs 2.5%; at standard tier it is neutral (97.5% vs 100%); its edge is armour piercing and x1.25, not Strain. **Longer fights**: levels and tier change the win rate, not the length (median 2 at levels 2-8); the lever is the encounter's `total_blocks`: 4 blocks elite L2 42.5% / median 3 (control 0% / 4); 3 blocks elite L2 12.5% / 7 (0% / 8); 3 blocks with plate and kite shield, elite L4, 20% / 8 (0% / 10). **Flesh-Tension trap**: no signal (0 poise breaks in the duel; against `tl_enforcer` at 4 blocks Channel 92.5% with 8 breaks vs control 60% with 18; breaks are not split by side). **Feed under Acute Resonance**: never observed (Feed always at 80+ Strain, healing 10-12, i.e. 4%; the -25% applies only at 25-49). **No regressions**: `sim feel 5 seeds=40` 61.0% (identical to R4-CLOSE); `sim party 5` 12 seeds identical to R4-CLOSE; 40 seeds 100 / 93 / 100 / 95 / 80, 0 METRIC FAIL; the `channel` rule is inert for non-holders.
- **Recommendation**: (a) data (recommended, the asset lane): give `inoculated_hero` one marked marrow attack active (`tl_hook_thrust` in `extra_skills`, `deck.active` and `deck.marrow`) and give `pk1_channel_duel` `total_blocks: 3` or 4 so fights last; (b) the crisis: Option A (recommended): run the channel meter's `round_end` triggers before decay (the crisis then fires at 100 as CANON 2.2 says), a kit order change behind the existing rules (golden-safe: no shipped holder outside the test fight); Option B: the trigger reads `at_least 90`; (c) decide whether the edge x power cliff is intended (a smoother AI rule or numbers); (d) Strain's downside and Channel against elites: the levers are Edge's empower, the recoil percent, decay, band thresholds, Vent's HP cost, `channel_power`, `channel_cost` and the crisis table (a tuning pass once the crisis fires); (e) the AI never Vents: a Vent rule for the AI (for example near the max, or before a crisis) if the sim should measure it; (f) tools: `tune=` cannot reach the channel numbers and `counts=on` has no Strain counters (crisis, Vent, time in band, per-side poise breaks); a small sim row would help the next pass.

### FB-194: PR5-25 `SKILL-UNMARK-TURNED` landed (a SUPPORT `unmark` reaches a turned party member)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR5-25; `FB-187` (5) (the `PR5-25` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@ea3bf66f), follow-up `SKILL-UNMARK-TURNED`
- **Finding**: `mark::unmark_reaches` (one rule for `command::target_problem` and `item_side_problem`): an `unmark` skill or item used by a party-side unit reaches a battle-start party member now `hostile_all`; an enemy-side user never does; a skill without `unmark` still needs a friend. Under round_intent a Support round may take that member as its target while the unit holds a usable `unmark` skill (`mark::unmark_skills`: known, not used up) or an offered `unmark` item (`mark::unmark_round_target` in `round_intent::target_problem`), and the pick menu on the member lists those rows (`round_intent_plan::menu_rows_on`). **Beyond the row**: under round_intent the consumable `unmark` (Quenched Cinder Salt) could not reach a turned ally before (the Support round-target check refused it), although PK1 3.7 / FB-174 say it may; the same check fixes it. AI: a party-side unit plans its `unmark` skill on the first turned member (battle-start order) it would calm (`mark::would_calm`: by `sync_tracks`' formula the stage after the latest mark goes is no departure stage, so no unmark is spent on an overshoot; not already targeted by another unit's unmark): the first step of the classic draft (`ai::try_unmark`) and a Support round before any tactic under round_intent (`ai::unmark_pick`); deterministic, no draw; it early-returns with no side effect when no shipped skill has `unmark` (all of today's data). Tests: `rpgkit_battle/tests/skill_unmark_turned.rs` (5), `rpgkit_world/tests/unit_tracks.rs` (+1: calmed at Stage V, settles at Stage IV, nothing departs), `rpgkit_host/tests/unmark_turned.rs` (1). Combined gate (with PR5-23) 2923 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings; 0 golden diffs. Review: change-reviewer PASS (kit comments named game content: reworded generically at landing).
- **Recommendation**: `combat_hooks.md`: "SKILL-UNMARK-TURNED: a SUPPORT skill's `unmark` (as a consumable's) reaches a battle-start party member now `hostile_all` and calms it at the next round start; under round_intent a Support round may target that member while the unit holds a usable `unmark` skill or item; the party AI uses its `unmark` skill on a turned member it would calm." `kits/rpg/README.md`: done. Questions (built defaults): (a) a SUPPORT skill combining `unmark` with heal, grant or cleanse applies all its parts to the hostile member (as an `unmark` item that also heals does); alternative: only the `unmark` part when the target is not a friend; (b) the classic draft may still strike the member it is calming this round (`rank_targets` over `foes_of`, since PR5-8); alternative: a turned member being calmed is no target; (c) the AI does not walk to an out-of-reach turned member, and uses the skill only, not the Salt. Test follow-up (low): the "never the foe's" check in `the_ai_calms_the_turned_member_when_the_unmark_would` should give the foe the skill too (it rides with PR5-23's landing).

### FB-195: PR5-23 `MUSICVAR1` landed (music variants, Ogg loop points)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR5-23; ART_UI_STUDIO 3.9 requests 1-2 (the `PR5-23` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@ea3bf66f), follow-up `MUSICVAR1`
- **Finding**: Engine (`engine/crates/aegis_ui/src/audio.rs`, game-agnostic): a track with numbered variants beside it (`<key>_2.ogg`, `<key>_3.ogg`, ... in the folder that holds `<key>.ogg`; missing numbers skipped) is a pool (`Audio::music_variants`, listed through `vfs::list_dir`, cached until the next mount); `Audio::music_variant(rel, request, seed)` picks one per request from its own stream (`pick_variant`: a fresh `GodotRng` seeded from `hash([seed, "music_variant"])`, `VARIANT_STREAM`; no gameplay roll moves, `rng_hash` unchanged), keeps it for that request (a game id; without one, while the pick is the wanted music and not after it has played and something else was wanted) and never repeats the previous pick. Loop points: `loop_points(bytes)` reads `LOOPSTART` / `LOOPLENGTH` (sample frames) from the Ogg Vorbis comment header itself (rodio and lewton do not expose it; every length uses `checked_add`, and the last page's granule is checked against the file size), `IntroLoop` plays the intro once and loops the span sample-exact (no seeking; reserves at most 4 Mi samples up front); untagged files, WAVs and malformed or unusable tags loop whole as before; the same on the web. Game (`emberwake_gui`): `music::battle_track` (one variant per battle, seeded by the battle's seed; the next battle never repeats it), `music::track` elsewhere, `Battle.music_request`; the shipped `sunk_ward_combat`, `_2`, `_3` now play as a pool. Tests: `aegis_ui/tests/music_variants.rs` (7), `music_loops.rs` (7, with two 1 s ffmpeg fixtures in `aegis_ui/tests/fixtures/music/`, malformed headers and hostile granules), GUI `battle/tests/music_tests.rs` and a `music` unit test. Also here: the PR5-25 test follow-up (the foe knows the unmark too, FB-194). Gate 2926 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings; 0 golden diffs. Review: change-reviewer, 2 medium (an unnamed request reused an old pick; an untrusted span size could abort) and 2 low fixed.
- **Recommendation**: engine/README.md, PORT-AUDIO paragraph (~:132): after "one looping music track that crossfades (1 s; 2 s from silence; 6 dB under the effects)" add "; MUSICVAR1: a track with numbered variants beside it (`<key>_2.ogg`, `<key>_3.ogg`, ... in the same folder; missing numbers skipped) is a pool, and `Audio::music_variant(rel, request, seed)` picks one per request from its own seeded stream (`pick_variant`, `VARIANT_STREAM`; no gameplay roll moves), keeps it for that request and never repeats the previous pick; listed through `vfs::list_dir`, so the web bundle's files count. An Ogg Vorbis track tagged `LOOPSTART` / `LOOPLENGTH` (sample frames; `loop_points` reads the comment header itself, checking every length and the last granule against the file size) plays its intro once and loops the span sample-exact (`IntroLoop`, no seeking, the same on the web); untagged files, WAVs and malformed tags loop whole as before"; after "Emberwake picks tracks with the MUSIC1 chain (...)" add "and one variant per battle (`music::battle_track`, seeded by the battle's seed; `music::track` elsewhere)"; the `crates/aegis_ui/` table row: "music variants (`audio::music_variants` / `music_variant` / `pick_variant`) and loop points (`audio::loop_points` / `looped` / `IntroLoop`, MUSICVAR1)". GAME_BRIEF (music): "MUSICVAR1: a battle plays one variant of its track per battle (never the last battle's pick); variant files `<key>_<n>.ogg` sit beside `<key>.ogg`; loop points from `LOOPSTART` / `LOOPLENGTH` tags." STRUCTURE: `aegis_ui/tests/{music_variants,music_loops}.rs`, `aegis_ui/tests/fixtures/music/`, `emberwake_gui` `battle/tests/music_tests.rs`. `games/emberwake/assets/audio/music/README.md` (Art/UI): replace "they play only once the engine picks among a key's `<key>_N` variants" with "the engine picks one per battle (MUSICVAR1); a track may carry `LOOPSTART` / `LOOPLENGTH` Vorbis comments (sample frames) to play its intro once and loop the span". Readings: no shipped track has loop tags yet (every track loops whole until the music tool writes them); a key ending in `_<digits>` would count as a variant of the shorter key (none ships); the agent link's `new` keeps the current variant (Restart picks a new one).

### FB-197: PR5-26 `AUDIT-PR5-BUGS` landed (audit 1A fixes in five groups)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR5-26; `docs/audits/2026-11-10_0de2c60_consistency_audit.md` 1A; `FB-191` (b), (e), (f); `FB-193` (a) (the `PR5-26` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: open
- **Finding**: (1) `Command::into_rest` clears the skill id and the marrow flag (a coerced Rest no longer pays or counts as Feed). (2) Forbidden two-handed defaults: `unit::row_slot_item` (one rule with `equip`), `UnitState::default_item` / `forbids_default`; `validate` and `sanitize_loadout` accept `NO_ITEM` (`"none"`) in a known slot; `build_as` keeps it empty; `roster::stash_forbidden` stashes a worn forbidden item and writes `"none"` in a slot whose default is forbidden (the default is never stashed), reported in the new settle key `cleared` [{member, slot}] (only when non-empty; `PARTY_KEYS` 9) and a Results line `X's main hand is empty: X can't wield ITEM now.`; the Dojo (`eligible`, `equip_internal`) and the party screen (`equip`, `take_off`, `unequip`, `move_slot`, all in the played world) refuse what `Unit::forbids`. (3) An automatic deck's marrow marks must be among its automatic actives under every channel ruleset with the deck rule (`check/unit.rs` `marrow_errors`, `state.rs` `deck_pass` via `progression::build_unchecked`); the party screen keeps, prunes and saves `deck.marrow`. (4) `Command::pierces` (the hit forecast) includes `pierce_melee` on melee Attack and Area hits, as `land`; the Dojo hit and planner notes too; `damage::weapon_for(u, false)` reads `active_weapon()` (differs only in the Dojo's weapon toggle); GUI: hazard meter lines (`battle/marks.rs` "+15 Strain"), Channel Rest and marrow-version notes on strip cards (`battle/tips.rs`), `tracks` / `stashed` / `cleared` on the Results screen (`hub.rs`); `unit_tracks.name` must be set; `rpgkit_host` exposes PK state only when set (`state_of` per unit: `channel`, `marrow`, `meters`, `marks`, `skill_uses`, `tracks`, `forbid_hands`, `pierce_melee`; `outcome_of`; `skills_of`: `uses_per_battle`, `uses_left`, `unmark`, `channel_only`, `marrow`; `round_plan` `channel`). (5) Round end runs the `round_end` triggers before the unit meters' decay (FB-191 Option A; the decay reads the round's guards from before the triggers), so the crisis at 100 fires; the AI's Channel row counts a marrow-only attack (`marrow_planned`); a Vent row from 75% of the channel meter's max (`CHANNEL_AI_VENT_PCT`, `vent_row`: not on Economy opener-only rounds; returns the Vent's own pick); sim: `seeds=` holds in party and sweep (`args.rs`), `tune=` takes `channel_power`, `channel_ai_edge`, `channel_cost_1..3`, `counts=on` adds marrow actions, Vents, Channel rounds, channel meter triggers, rounds by band and breaks taken per side; `tools/fc.sh` and `tools/tiles/board.py` use `RUSTUP_TOOLCHAIN=stable` off Windows when unset. **Correction to the row**: shipped data does hold Strain (`inoculated_hero`, `aberrant_test` in `pk1_channel_duel` and the Dojo preset), so that fight's behaviour changes (the intended FB-191 A); no golden runs it. One test relaxed with reason (`an_enemy_holder_channels_and_its_tells_say_so` accepts a marrow-only or Vent round, which carry no marrow line). Tests: about 20 new or extended across battle, rules, world, content, sim, host, GUI and `test_tiles.py`. Gate 2951 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings; 0 golden diffs. Review: change-reviewer PASS, 1 medium (`move_slot` could stash `"none"`) and 5 low fixed.
- **Recommendation**: `combat_hooks.md`: "round end runs the `round_end` triggers before the unit meters' decay, so a meter's crisis `at_least` its max fires first (a trigger that raises a meter now lands before decay and can count as gained); the AI Vents from 75% of the channel meter's max; its Channel row counts a marrow-only attack." GAME_BRIEF (PK2 tracks): "a member whose stage forbids two-handed items gets `none` in a slot whose two-handed default it cannot wield (the default is not stashed; reported as `cleared`); the slot stays empty until gear is equipped." Sim notes / BALANCE: the new `tune=` keys, `counts=on` counters, `seeds=` in party / sweep. Tools / cloud workflow: the `RUSTUP_TOOLCHAIN` default. `kits/rpg/README.md`: done. No HISTORY row.

Content check clean; 2951 tests passed, 0 failed; 0 golden diffs (no golden runs this fight). `BALANCE_REPORT.md` has the dated PR5-27 entry.

### FB-201: PR5-27 balance rerun: the AI never Vents (a PR5-26 bug), crises do fire, Vent's numbers need a call

- **Spec**: READY row PR5-27 part (3) and acceptance ("Channel rounds, crises, and Vent in 40-seed `sim feel counts=on`"); PK1 6.1
- **Kind**: sim
- **Status**: open
- **Finding**: Channel rounds: yes (97 in 40 fights). **Crises: yes, but the sim does not show them**: 40 `aegis battle` logs hold 55 crises (1.38 a fight, every fight at least one; Strain 25 -> 65 -> 85 after Feed, Strain Bite makes 100, the crisis resets to 50, decay to 40); the `counts=on` "rounds by band" samples after the PR5-26 `round_end` reset, so Rupture reads 0 (a sim counter gap, PR5-26's). **Vent: never**, a PR5-26 bug: `ai::vent_row` plans `(Support, Myself)`, which `round_intent::target_problem` refuses under `field_intent` (every timeline ruleset; a self skill is a Guard/Stand pick there, as in `self_stand_picks`), so `commit` fails at every Strain 75-99 and the Channel row then refuses a round reaching 100; the kit test passes only because its ruleset lacks `field_intent`. Build is fixing both (PR5-26 follow-up: the Vent pick shape under `field_intent` with a timeline-ruleset test, and a crises counter). With the fix and today's Vent numbers (-20 Strain, 15% HP, a round of Vent plus two Feed blocks: net -10 and no attack), the AI Vents at every 85 and never attacks: 2.9 Vents a fight, 0 crises, 0% won. Watch list (production data, no fix; 40 seeds; Channel vs `rules=-channel`): won 22.5% vs 0%, median 6 vs 8 rounds, Feed 55 uses x 10 HP (4% of 262 cut by Acute Resonance), Strain self-damage from Edge hits 696 HP over 40 fights, 4 of 6 battle wins end at 25 HP or less, 0 breaks taken (Flesh-Tension not exercised: no poise attacker; the 50-74 band is passed within a round). Vent and crises together, on top of the fix (40 battle logs each): Vent -50 / `hp_cost_pct` 10: 1 Vent and 0.93 crises a fight, won 37.5%; -50 / 15: 0.78 crises, 30%; -50 / 5: 0.95, 40%; -45 / 10: 0.93, 35%; -35 / 10 with Bite 10: 0.93, 37.5%; -60 / 10: 0 crises, 7.5% (flips); -35 or -40 alone: 0 crises, 0%; AI Vent threshold 90%: 0.15 Vents, 1.38 crises, 17.5%; 95%: no Vents. `channel_power` (production, no fix): 1.25 22.5%, 1.5 45%, 1.75 60%, 2.0 85%; `channel_ai_edge` 1.0 same as 1.2, 1.5 never Channels (the known cliff).
- **Recommendation**: (a) Vent's numbers (PK1 4.3 item 5 says -20 Strain, 15% HP): Option A (recommended): -50 Strain, `hp_cost_pct` 10 (both mechanics show; 37.5% against this elite); Option B: keep -20 / 15 and change the Vent round's shape (Vent as the opener only, the rest of the round attacks, not Feed padding; a kit row, untested); Option C: AI Vent threshold to 90% (a kit number; Vent rare). The result is knife-edge (-60 flips), so pick deliberately. (b) `channel_power` stays 1.25 until Vent is settled (1.4-1.5 is the lever for ~50% against this elite; a global rule number). (c) A Strain-free hero control (the Bite rider off) would be a fairer baseline than `rules=-channel`. (d) Flesh-Tension needs a poise attacker in the test fight to be measured.

### FB-202: PR5-26 follow-up landed: the AI Vents under `field_intent`; `counts=on` counts crises

- **Spec**: READY row PR5-26 group (5) ("an AI Vent rule at high Strain", "Strain/crisis/Vent counters to `counts=on`"); found by the PR5-27 balance run (FB-201)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: open
- **Finding**: `ai::vent_row` now plans Vent on itself in the row the planner gives a self pick of the skill (`round_intent_plan::pick_row_at`, now `pub(crate)`): Stand (Guard) under `field_intent` (IR15: a self target is Stand's; Vent derives Stand), Support otherwise; no special case for Vent's id. Before, its `(Support, Myself)` pick was refused under `field_intent` (every shipped timeline ruleset), so the AI never Vented; the kit test now runs on both rulesets (and fails on the old pick). The sim's `counts=on` adds a per-side `crises` counter (the channel meter's `round_end` rows with `when.meter at_least` at or above the holder's max, counted as they fire: `trigger::State::battle_count`); the existing "channel meter triggers" counter also counts the Edge recoil (`hit_dealt`) row, so it was never a crisis count. `sim feel 2 seeds=40 enc=pk1_channel_duel ruleset=timeline counts=on`: party Vents 116 (2.9 a fight), Channel rounds 159, crises 3, won 0%, median 5 rounds (with today's Vent numbers the AI Vents at every 75+ and stops attacking: FB-201's question). Gate 2952 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings; 0 golden diffs (plain sim output unchanged). known-issues: a row for linker errors after the disk fills mid-build (`cargo clean -p <crate>`).
- **Recommendation**: `combat_hooks.md` (PR5-26 line): "the AI's Vent pick takes the planner's self row (Stand under `field_intent`)". Sim notes: `counts=on` `crises`; the "channel meter triggers" label counts every channel-meter trigger row (rename or narrow it if wanted). `kits/rpg/README.md`: done.

- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: open

No shipped family has `range_band`, so play is unchanged. (3) `schema.json` help (exact-replace, valid JSON): `block_cost` (it sits in `item_families`, not `items.tier`) dropped from the `base` help and from `adjustable`'s default and options (retired in U3.2-S1; the loader already strips it; the option removal is a builder reading); `engine_rules` help names `reach` and points to `range_band`; `CAMPAIGN1-DOC` (in `portrait_cues.trigger` and the world meters' `scope`, not `unit_tracks`) now says it was refused and that per-member stages are `unit_tracks` rows (the check message at `check/campaign.rs:288` still names it); PHASEFX1 `side` / `swap` already documented (no change). Tests: `rpgkit_content/tests/family_range_band.rs` (3), `rpgkit_battle/tests/reach.rs` `the_reach_band_edge_is_the_family_range_band_max` (edges 2, 3, 4 and the fallback). Gate 2956 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings; 0 golden diffs. Review: planner (no behaviour change; the gate and goldens confirm).
- **Recommendation**: `kits/rpg/README.md` (gear): "PR5-28: a family's optional `range_band {min: 1, max: 2-6}` (reach families only) sets the reach rule's melee band edge (the loader carries it as the derived item key `family_reach_max`); without it, `REACH_WEAPON_REACH = 2`." Questions: (a) give polearm and scourge `range_band {min: 1, max: 2}` by seed now (no change in play; note scourge's item band `bands.main_1h.range.max` is 1 and is not the reach); (b) `round_intent::melee_band` (attack lock, diagonal test) still treats a band past 2 as not melee: if a family ever goes above 2, should it read the unit's reach.

### FB-196: PR5-24 `STORYMUSIC1` landed (story steps and vignettes ask for music; the game's `music_keys` in `game.json`)

- **Spec**: READY Section 1 row PR5-24; ART_UI_STUDIO 3.9 request 3 (the `PR5-24` squash commit on `develop`, 2026-11-10)
- **Kind**: docs-delta
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@859f9d40), follow-up `STORYMUSIC1`
- **Finding**: A vignette's `music` and a new optional `music` on every sequence step kind read the same way (`rpgkit_data` `MusicAsk`, `music_ask`): `""` keeps the music, `none` is silence, a path ending `.ogg` plays as written, any other value is a music key. The content check (`check/story.rs` `music_error`) accepts a key the game lists in `game.json` **`music_keys`** (new optional manifest key, read like the purse caps: `source::MUSIC_KEYS`, in `MANIFEST_KEYS`, `music_keys_problems` refuses a non-list, a non-id or a key twice; `View::music_keys`) or a key whose `audio/music/<key>.ogg` the asset layers hold; an unknown or non-id key is one error, a missing `.ogg` path a warning. Emberwake's `game.json` lists its 9 MUSIC1 contexts and the story keys `story_opening`, `prologue_tension`, `story_escape`, `story_goodbye` (set with `tools/fc.sh content manifest`); the kit names none of them (review M1: an earlier draft held both lists as kit constants). The game resolves a key through its music chain (`music::game_track`: region-scoped file first, then `<key>.ogg`; MUSICVAR1 variants apply); with no track the music goes on. On the story stage a step's music becomes the stage's music when the stage reaches it, before its vignettes (`StoryStage::take_step_music`); an `effects` step's music carries to the next step the stage plays (`rpgkit_world::story::step_music`: the nearest one wins); a vignette's own music still wins while it plays; a `retry` fight does not re-apply it. Tests: `rpgkit_world/tests/story_music.rs` (2), `rpgkit_content/tests/story_checks.rs` (+2: the manifest key, keys or tracks), `emberwake_gui` story test (+1, with `game.json`'s list holding every `music.rs` context). Gate 2931 passed, 0 failed, 4 ignored, 0 warnings; 0 golden diffs. Review: change-reviewer PASS (M1 layer: fixed by the manifest key; L1 a tautological test expectation: fixed).
- **Recommendation**: confirm the new `game.json` key. GAME_BRIEF (manifest keys / story music): "`game.json` `music_keys` (optional, STORYMUSIC1): the music keys a vignette's or a sequence step's `music` may name without a track: Emberwake's 9 MUSIC1 contexts and `story_opening`, `prologue_tension`, `story_escape`, `story_goodbye`; set with `aegis content manifest`; without it only a shipped `audio/music/<key>.ogg` counts. A step's music holds from that step on on the story stage (an effects step's from the next step played); a vignette's own music wins while it plays." STRUCTURE: test `kits/rpg/crates/rpgkit_world/tests/story_music.rs`. Art/UI (music README, ART_UI_STUDIO 3.9): story tracks go in `games/emberwake/assets/audio/music/story_opening.ogg` (and the other three), or a region-scoped `<region>_story_opening.ogg` (AI-03 names `assets/music/`, but the player reads `assets/audio/music/`); data wiring sets `music` on the vignette row or the step. `kits/rpg/README.md`: done. Notes (built defaults): loading a save mid-sequence starts from the menu theme plus the current step's own or carried music (music from an earlier visible step is not restored); `music` on `enter` and `end` steps has no audible effect, and on a `fight` step it is the music after the fight; the story keys have no parent context (no track: the music goes on).

### FB-198: PR5-26 clause B9 parked (per-rank `hp_cost_pct`, `uses_per_battle`) and four questions

- **Spec**: READY row PR5-26 group (4) "add `hp_cost_pct` & `uses_per_battle` to `source.rs` `RANK_KEYS`" (audit B9)
- **Kind**: limit-blocker
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@859f9d40), follow-up `AUDIT-PR5-FOLLOWUP`
- **Finding**: Not built: `kits/rpg/schema.json` marks both fields `"rank": false`, and `channel_checks.rs` (:93-95) and `skill_unmark_checks.rs` (:76-86) pin "not per rank", as PK1-S2a and PK2-S1 specify; adding them to `RANK_KEYS` alone would split the schema from the code. Today an unknown key in a rank modifier is silently dropped, which is likely what B9 saw. Questions on built defaults: (Q2) after the crisis sets Strain to 50 the decay still runs in a round without gain, so Strain ends at 40; (Q3) the AI Vent threshold (75% of the meter's max) is a kit constant; (Q4) a slot emptied as `"none"` stays empty after the track stage falls (the player equips something); (Q5) `UnitState::deck_errors` builds in the member's own world (from its `lore_ref`), as `validate` has no played world.
- **Recommendation**: B9: Option A: make both per rank (`RANK_KEYS` 26 -> 28, schema `"rank": true`, flip the two tests); Option B (recommended): keep them not per rank and have the check refuse a non-rank key in a rank modifier (no silent drop). Q2: Option A (recommended): a meter set by a `round_end` trigger skips that round's decay (Strain stays 50); Option B: keep 40. Q3: keep the constant, or a ruleset number `channel_ai_vent_pct` (a named contract re-record). Q4: keep (default), or sanitize drops the marker once the default is allowed again. Q5: keep.

### FB-199: Build process error: the PR5-24 landing reverted Design's newer docs (fixed by Design @d2a94371)

- **Spec**: Build landing procedure (cloud)
- **Kind**: suggestion
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@859f9d40)
- **Finding**: The PR5-24 squash (`94cc7f86`) was built by applying `git diff origin/develop <task branch> -- engine games kits tools` onto `develop`. The task branch did not hold Design's newer commits (`c8091291`, `3fa8e999`), so that diff reverted their changes under `games/emberwake/docs/` (lore, specs) and `engine/docs/`; Design restored them in `d2a94371`. The same step would have reverted `8e0926b1` and `d2a94371` in this landing; caught before the push (the staged file list showed docs it should not touch). From this landing on, a squash applies only the task's own diff, from the branch's base sync commit to its head (`git diff <base> <head>`), and the staged file list is checked for paths outside the task. Earlier landings this session applied the same pattern while the branch held `develop` (no newer Design commits), so they reverted nothing; PR5-24 is the only one.
- **Recommendation**: known-issues gains the row (this push). Design may want to re-check `games/emberwake/docs/` and `engine/docs/` against its own last commits once (d2a94371 restored what Design found).

### FB-204: PR5-28 item (1) parked: `spell_reach` is live (`wpn_orb` +1), retiring it moves goldens

- **Spec**: READY row PR5-28 item (1) ("retire `spell_reach` ... migrating `wpn_orb` ... to `range_band`"); audit 1B, FB-25, U4 call it dead
- **Kind**: limit-blocker
- **Status**: closed: accepted (@859f9d40), follow-up `R14-SPEC-CLEANUP`
- **Finding**: Not built. `spell_reach` is live: spell skills add `gear_spell_reach.min(GEAR_REACH_CAP = 1) + passive_spell_reach` to their reach (`rpgkit_battle/src/skill.rs:302` `band()`, `Unit::spell_reach_bonus` `rpgkit_rules/src/unit.rs:1723`; shapes cast from the user's own tile get none), and `wpn_orb` (+1) is the only shipped item using it, matching its description. With `spell_reach_bonus` at 0 (a reverted experiment) `sim_golden` breaks (13 of 22 runs; its frozen catalog `timeline_kit.json` holds `wpn_orb` with `spell_reach 1`); a retirement would also move `rules_contract_golden` (line 83, `locked budget.rs::W_SPELL_REACH gear`) and `checks_golden` (lines 512 and 671: the "unknown effect type" lists name `spell_reach`; the lists live in `check/simple.rs:74, 83, 195`, not `check/hooks.rs`). `range_band` cannot stand in: it is a melee reach family's band (PR5-28 item 2) with no meaning for an off-hand focus.
- **Recommendation**: Option C (recommended): keep `spell_reach`, correct the "dead" note in the audit, FB-25 and U4, and drop item (1) from PR5-28. Option A: define a focus's reach (a new focus field, a loader that converts the old effect so the frozen catalog still reads +1): 0 sim diffs, but `checks_golden` and `rules_contract_golden` re-recorded. Option B: retire it outright: the orb loses its +1 and its description changes; `sim_golden` (13 runs), `checks_golden` (2 lines) and `rules_contract_golden` (1 line) re-recorded with HISTORY rows.

RPG nouns in engine `.rs` (lines / files): battle 95 / 18, round 269 / 50, block 211 / 27, encounter 16 / 3, ruleset 20 / 2; the public names that are file formats are `aegis_cli::scenario` `Launch.encounter` / `ruleset` (scenario JSON keys and CLI args; 2 game scenario files; `tools/fc.sh scenario shots`) and `aegis_host::playtest` `battles` (the recording format under `games/emberwake/playtests`); `round` and `block` need a per-hit audit (many are maths or UI uses).
